Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any bio module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 757. The projective resolution has length at most 44 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the bio decomposition. By minimality, the element a^757 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 44-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
